PhD program
Home » Program » PhD program
Every year, Chimie ParisTech - PSL welcomes around a hundred PhD students in its research teams, distributed across its 3 laboratories.
The PhD is the highest university degree. This PhD is recognized worldwide and is practically required for professionals who want to work in R&D, especially in the chemical and pharmaceutical industries.
Training PhD students through research allows them to acquire project management skills on top of technical skills. Doing a thesis at Chimie ParisTech - PSL is particularly relevant given the proximity of the school to the industrial world. In addition to their scientific education, PhD students acquire training in innovation and a knowledge of the socio-economic world. Of the approximately one hundred students doing their thesis at Chimie ParisTech - PSL, 25% are financed by the industry (CEA, ADEME, IFP, companies, etc.), of which 16% are under a CIFRE agreement. This shows how close research laboratories are to companies.
PhD schools
ED 397 Physics and Chemistry of Materials
ED 388 Physical Chemistry and Analytical Chemistry of Paris Centre
ED 621 ISMME Systems, Materials, Mechanics and Energy Engineering
ED406 Molecular chemistry Paris Centre
PhD schools offer compulsory training in addition to the research work. This program allows students to learn how to manage innovation, bibliographies, etc.
It also supports PhD students with their thesis by monitoring them and their work throughout the thesis with a yearly review and assigning them a teacher-researcher who will be responsible for the supervision of their thesis.
Doing a thesis at Chimie ParisTech
Students must contact each research team manager based on the research subject they have chosen
The vast majority of the thesis Chimie ParisTech - PSL offers are published on the websites of the doctoral schools and the school’s research laboratories:
Chemistry Research Institute of Paris (IRCP), CNRS Chimie ParisTech - PSL UMR 8247
Institute of Chemistry for Life and Health Sciences (i-CLeHS) CNRS Chimie ParisTech - PSL FRE 2027
Institut Photovoltaïque d’Ile de France (IPVF) UMR 9006
International joint supervision PhD programs
The school develops international joint supervision theses. As a symbol of excellent and lasting scientific relations, joint supervision PhD students benefit from the best assets of both partners.
Since 2018, six joint supervision programs have been set up, notably with Mexico, China, South Africa, Italy and Canada.
Procedure
PhD students are registered in both institutions and carry out their work under the responsibility of a thesis manager in each country. Students work on their thesis while splitting their time between the institutions concerned based on a schedule and terms predefined in the joint supervision agreement.
The language in which the thesis is written is defined in this agreement. When this language is not French, a substantial summary in French needs to be added to the paper. The title of Doctor is conferred simultaneously and jointly by each institution concerned and they each issue their own PhD degree to the student after a single thesis defense.
The establishment of a joint supervision thesis is up to its future managers. Chimie ParisTech - PSL researchers are invited to contact the International Relations Department to set it up.
International co-management of the thesis
If the thesis is internationally co-managed, PhD students carry out their research work under the responsibility of two thesis managers, one from the institution where they are registered and another from the partner. A co-management agreement may be signed by both institutions and the research directors concerned.
PhD students are enrolled in only one institution, from which they will receive the rank of doctor. A single defense takes place at the university in which the students are enrolled.
For a PhD student enrolled in France, the thesis is usually written in French. This international co-management system allows the PhD student to benefit from a joint supervision.
Financing
PhD students have 3 years to complete their thesis in France. During this period, they retain the status of student while receiving a remuneration that gives them an employee status (social security contribution, retirement, unemployment). This remuneration may be:
A PhD contract from the Ministry of Higher Education for Research and Innovation.
A Convention Industrielle de Formation par la Recherche (CIFRE, “Industrial Convention of Formation by Research”) associated with hiring by a company.
A training contract through research (CFR in partnership with the CEA, etc.)
A research contract (ANR, Europe, Ademe, DGA, foundations, etc.).
A scholarship from a foreign government. For example, ParisTech and the China Scholarship Council (CSC) signed a PhD program agreement in France in 2011. This program is open to highly qualified Chinese students who want to pursue their PhD education in a ParisTech school. The funding granted covers a period ranging from 36 to 48 months in France. From 2013 to 2018, 27 students were admitted to the program to do their PhD at Chimie ParisTech. Students also have the opportunity to carry out a co-supervised PhD as part of a collaboration between the research group they come from in China and their host laboratory in France, with a CSC scholarship covering 6 to 24 months in France.
Thesis registration
Students must first have the agreement of the PhD school and of the thesis director to proceed with their registration. The Chimie ParisTech - PSL thesis department takes care of the administrative registration or re-registration of PhD students carrying out their thesis at Chimie ParisTech - PSL.

Director of Research
Chimie ParisTech - PSL, France’s leading engineering school specializing in chemistry
Chimie ParisTech - PSL has been training chemical and general engineers for the past 120 years. Its founder, Charles Friedel, wanted future chemical entrepreneurs to be trained in contact with research while in college. Chimie ParisTech - PSL therefore hosts a research center that is active and recognized in various fields of chemistry: materials, processes, chemistry for the living, molecular chemistry and covers fields such as health, energy, environment and mobility

Computer Science

Print
Inspired by international graduate schools, the Computer Science graduate programme brings together the strengths of PSL University at the master's and doctoral levels. By bringing together stakeholders from the various PSL institutions (Dauphine-PSL, ENS-PSL), PRAIRIE institute and PSAI, the Computer Science graduate programme develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalised study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalisation of courses and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programmes strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programmes and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.

PhD Track
PSL PhD tracks | Join outstanding students from around the globe and launch your world-class research career with the PSL Graduate programme
The highly selective PhD tracks at PSL University prepare students for research careers in France or abroad, in academia or industry.
These five-year programmes are designed for high-potential students who want to tackle the scientific challenges of tomorrow and prepare their research project from the Master's level onwards. Immersed in the ecosystem of excellence of PSL's Computer Science graduate programme (top-level laboratories and academic teams, international partners), successful applicants benefit from a personalised course of study geared towards their thesis topic from the first year of their Master's degree.
Why join the PSL PhD track?
Curriculum of excellence (Master's + PhD) accessible in M1
Funding for the entire duration of studies (5 years) & welcome package in year 1
Courses taught entirely in English
Close supervision & support for professional integration
Immersion in a multicultural & cutting-edge scientific environment in the heart of Paris
Find out more (admissions, scholarships, etc.)
Discover the PhD track & apply
Education
Research
Choose your Master’s degree and define your academic path
The graduate program offers the following Master’s degree:
Master's degree in Computer Science
Personalized course offerings
Data Science Program
Data Science Program
Complementary university-wide courses
Graduate program students at both the Master’s and PhD level have access to university-wide trainings in core competencies, both introductory and advanced, designed to supplement their academic study.
Those complementary university-wide courses, available in all of PSL’s component schools, are eligible for ECTS credit.

Inspired by international graduate schools, the Physics graduate programme brings together the strengths of PSL University at the master's and doctoral levels in physics. By networking the actors of the various PSL institutions (ESPCI Paris-PSL, ENS-PSL, Paris Observatory-PSL, Institut Curie, Collège de France), the Physics graduate programme develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalised study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalisation of curricula and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programmes strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programmes and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.

PHD Track

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
ED Cognitive Science
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Presentation :
The PSL University Doctoral School of Cognitive Science was born out of the ambition to create a competitive doctoral school at the highest national and international level, supported by teams conducting cutting-edge research in cognitive science at the frontier of knowledge and its applications in society, and providing doctoral students with a solid conceptual and methodological foundation that will be an asset for their future integration into the professional world. The Department of Cognitive Studies at the École Normale Supérieure is a unique, interdisciplinary department for fundamental and applied research and training in cognitive science. It is located at the crossroads of the humanities, social sciences, life sciences, engineering, and computer science. The Department's 50 high-level HDR researchers study the full range of human cognition, integrating a wide array of theoretical tools and experimental techniques.

Journée de Chimie Moléculaire 2026

À propos de l'ED 406
L'ED 406 a pour but de préparer au plus haut niveau des étudiants à une activité professionnelle dans le domaine de la chimie moléculaire en utilisant les formidables atouts dont bénéficie Paris Centre.
Ça se passe à l'ED 406

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
International students
Home
Education
International students
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Embarking on the journey to study abroad can feel overwhelming, but we’re here to support you every step of the way in turning your dreams into reality. Take the first step with us, and get ready to unlock new possibilities at one of the world’s top universities!
Step 1 | Explore study opportunities at PSL
Search engine
Find programs based on your specific criteria
Training fields map
Explore our programs across six multidisciplinary training fields
Master's taught in English
Discover our programs offered entirely or partially in English
Aspiring to pursue a research-oriented career? Explore PSL PhD tracks: a highly selective, fully funded, 5-year Master's-PhD program taught entirely in English.
Step 2 | Learn about tuition fees at PSL
At PSL, you'll receive a world-class education with minimal financial burden. You can find information about tuition fees on each program's page, under the "Admissions" tab.
Step 3 | Explore scholarship opportunities tailored to your study plan
Explore the multiple scholarship opportunities PSL offers for both French and international students, and find the one that best supports your academic ambitions!
Step 4 | Get informed about the admissions procedures
Application platforms and deadlines at PSL vary depending on your degree program, nationality, and other criteria. Explore our dedicated pages below to find out when, where, and how to apply:
Apply to PSL Bachelorr's programs
Apply to PSL Master's programs
International admissions procedures at PSL
Step 5 | Discover why PSL is the ideal match for you
Now that you’ve explored all the exciting opportunities at PSL, you might still have questions: Is PSL the right fit for me? Why study in France? Don’t worry—we’re here to help!
Check out our guide to PSL and studying in France to discover what makes PSL exceptional and understand how higher education works in France.
Chat with our student ambassadors to hear directly from them—ask any questions you have!
Dive into our student blog for firsthand insights on academic and campus life, along with valuable admission tips.
Explore our student stories and learn about their academic experiences and journeys at PSL
Step 6 | Prepare for your journey
Congratulations on your admission to PSL! It’s time to prepare for your exciting journey, and we’re here to support you every step of the way as you begin this path to success.
The PSL Welcome Desk offers personalized assistance with all your administrative formalities, from visa applications and health insurance to other essential paperwork, both before and during your stay at PSL.
The PSL Housing Service is here to help you find the perfect place to live in Paris.
To enrich your experience, our Buddy Program and Tandem Program (language exchange) connect you with peers, offering a chance to immerse yourself in Parisian life and culture.
Got questions about campus life, living in Paris, or why students choose PSL?
Our students are here to chat and share their experiences!
You might find these blog posts interesting!

Ecole Doctorale Astronomie et Astrophysique d’Ile de France
mercredi 4 mars 2026
Toutes les informations sur le concours 2026 de l’Ecole doctorale sont disponibles dans le menu recrutement.
L’école doctorale en bref
L’École doctorale propose aux physiciens et mathématiciens une formation à et par la recherche dans le vaste domaine interdisciplinaire de l’astronomie et de toutes ses méthodes d’observation, de mesure et de calcul. Elle couvre un champ dont le développement est considérable et incessant : découverte des planètes extra-solaires, renouveau de la cosmologie à l’interface de la physique des particules, développement de l’astrochimie, exploration in situ du système solaire, navigation spatiale, planétologie à l’interface avec les sciences de la planète Terre. De puissants outils d’observation sont en préparation, aussi bien dans l’espace qu’au sol, affirmant la place de l’Europe, faisant appel à une grande diversité de technologies avancées (optique, métrologie, cryogénie, automatique...). Elle soutient également la préparation de thèses en instrumentation astronomique et astrophysique ouvertes, entre autres, aux jeunes diplômés en génie ingénieur.
Outre les métiers de la recherche en Europe et au-delà, les étudiants formés par un doctorat dans ces domaines ont acquis de solides compétences qui les qualifient pour des débouchés variés : enseignement supérieur, industries de pointe, informatique avancée, communication scientifique... L’École développe des interfaces avec les Écoles ou Formations doctorales de disciplines telles que mathématiques, informatique, optique, physique des plasmas, des particules, sciences de la Terre, etc.
Chaque année, l’Ecole doctorale accueille environ 50 nouveaux étudiants issus de Masters ou équivalent, sans condition de nationalité ni d’université d’origine. Ils préparent en 3 ans et en étant rémunérés par des contrats divers, un doctorat dans des laboratoires situés en Ile-de-France ou parfois à l’étranger.
Voir la fiche ADUM de l’ED.
Notre budget carbone
*Off topic mais important
Actualités
Résultats du concours 2026 de l’ED AAIF.
CD = Contrat Doctoral
Les candidats en liste principale vont recevoir un mail de l’école doctorale pour leur proposer un (ou un demi) CD. Ils auront une semaine pour l’accepter ou le décliner.
Les candidats en liste complémentaire pourront se voir proposer un CD ultérieurement en cas de désistement ou de financements complémentaires obtenus pour les projets de thèse en liste principale.

PhD admissions
Home » Admissions » PhD
PhD
Chimie ParisTech - PSL is recognized for its high-level research and accepts around one hundred PhD students in its laboratories, with nearly 40 theses being defended every year. The school has connections to four PhD schools that accompany PhD students throughout their 3-year thesis.
Chimie ParisTech - PSL develops international joint-supervision theses that allow PhD students to receive the title of doctor from each establishment. It is also possible to do a thesis under the joint supervision of two thesis supervisors.
To learn more
Registration and re-registration
Students must first hold a Master’s 2 and have the agreement of the PhD school and of the thesis director to register their thesis with Chimie ParisTech - PSL.
The Chimie ParisTech - PSL thesis department takes care of the administrative registration or re-registration of PSL PhD students carrying out their thesis at Chimie ParisTech.
The general registration procedure that needs to be followed on the platform can be found on the website.

Chimie ParisTech - PSL, France’s leading engineering school specializing in chemistry
Chimie ParisTech - PSL has been training chemical and general engineers for the past 120 years. Its founder, Charles Friedel, wanted future chemical entrepreneurs to be trained in contact with research while in college. Chimie ParisTech - PSL therefore hosts a research center that is active and recognized in various fields of chemistry: materials, processes, chemistry for the living, molecular chemistry and covers fields such as health, energy, environment and mobility

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
Astrophysics
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Inspired by international graduate schools, the Astrophysics graduate program brings together the strengths of PSL University at the master's and doctoral levels. By connecting stakeholders from different PSL institutions (Paris Observatory - PSL and ENS - PSL), the Astrophysics graduate program develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalized study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalization of curricula and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programs strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programs and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.
Key figures
285
Researchers and professors
50
Students in Master's programs
9
Laboratories
70
PhD students
Subjects
Sun, plasma
Planets, exoplanets
Stars and the interstellar medium
Galaxies and cosmology
Instrumental and digital engineering
Space engineering
High-energy astrophysics
Gravitation
PhD Track
PSL PhD tracks | Join outstanding students from around the globe & launch your world-class research career with PSL Graduate program
These five-year programs are designed for high-potential students who want to tackle the scientific challenges of tomorrow and prepare their research projects starting at the master's level. Immersed in the ecosystem of excellence offered by PSL's Astrophysics graduate program (top-level laboratories and academic teams, international partners), successful applicants benefit from a personalized course of study geared toward their thesis topic from the first year of their Master's degree.
Why join the PSL PhD track?
Curriculum of excellence (master's + doctorate) accessible in M1
Funding for the entire duration of studies (5 years) & welcome package in year 1
Courses taught entirely in English
Close supervision & support for professional integration
Immersion in a multicultural & cutting-edge scientific environment in the heart of Paris
More informations
Discover and candidate to the PhD track
Training
Search
Choose your Master’s degree and define your academic path
The graduate program offers the following Master’s degrees:
Master's degree in Space Science and Technology
Personalized course offerings
Complementary university-wide courses
Graduate program students at both the Master’s and PhD level have access to university-wide trainings in core competencies, both introductory and advanced, designed to supplement their academic study.
Those complementary university-wide courses, available in all of PSL’s component schools, are eligible for ECTS credit.

Career opportunities
Trained in and through research, PSL alumni find numerous employment opportunities in the public, private and entrepreneurial sectors as well as in the academic world.
Pursue a PhD
Doctoral research is conducted in a PSL laboratory. Training is conducted in a doctoral school.

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
Engineering (ISAI)
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Inspired by international graduate schools, the Engineering, Applied Sciences and Innovation (ISAI) graduate programme brings together the strengths of PSL University at master's and doctoral level, providing students with the scientific knowledge they need to tackle the major socio-economic and environmental challenges of tomorrow: energy transition, sustainable development, health, sustainable mobility and digital transition. By bringing together stakeholders from the various PSL institutions (Mines-PSL, ESPCI-PSL, Chimie Paris-PSL), the ISAI graduate programme develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalised study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalisation of curricula and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programmes strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programmes and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.
Key figures
250
Researchers and professors
1500
Students in Master's programs
14
Laboratories
360
PhD students
Subjects
Energy transition
Sustainable development
Health
Sustainable mobility
Issues in innovation
Digital transition
PHD TRACK
PSL PhD tracks | Join outstanding students from around the globe & launch your world-class research career with PSL Graduate programme
PSL University's highly selective PhD tracks prepare students for research careers in France or abroad, in academia or industry.
These five-year programmes are designed for high-potential students who want to tackle the scientific challenges of tomorrow and prepare their research project from the Master's level onwards. Immersed in the ecosystem of excellence of PSL's graduate engineering programme (top-level laboratories and academic teams, international partners), successful applicants benefit from a personalised course of study geared towards their thesis topic from the first year of their Master's degree.
Why join the PSL PhD track?
Curriculum of excellence (Master's + PhD) accessible in M1
Funding for the entire duration of studies (5 years) & welcome package in year 1
Courses taught entirely in English
Close supervision & support for professional integration
Immersion in a multicultural & cutting-edge scientific environment in the heart of Paris
Find out more (admissions, scholarships, etc.)
Discover the PhD track & apply
Eductaion
Research
Choose your Master’s degree and define your academic path
The graduate program offers the following Master’s degrees:
Master's degree in Materials Science and Engineering
Master's degree in BioMedical Engineering (ST4Health)
Engineering at École nationale supérieure de Chimie de Paris - PSL
Master's degree in Energy
Master's degree in Innovation management
Engineering at ESPCI Paris - PSL
Master's degree in Nuclear Energy
Engineering at MINES Paris - PSL
ISUPFERE Engineering at MINES Paris - PSL
Personalized course offerings
Complementary university-wide courses
Graduate program students at both the Master’s and PhD level have access to university-wide trainings in core competencies, both introductory and advanced, designed to supplement their academic study.
Those complementary university-wide courses, available in all of PSL’s component schools, are eligible for ECTS credit.

Career opportunities
Trained in and through research, PSL alumni find numerous employment opportunities in the public, private and entrepreneurial sectors as well as in the academic world.
Pursue a PhD
Doctoral research is conducted in a PSL laboratory. Training is conducted in a doctoral school.
Graduate program doctoral schools
École doctorale Ingénierie des Systèmes, Matériaux, Mécanique, Énergétique - ED 621
École doctorale Physique et chimie des Matériaux - ED 397
École doctorale Chimie physique et chimie analytique de Paris centre - ED 388
École doctorale Sciences mécaniques, acoustique, électronique et robotique - ED 391
École doctorale Chimie moléculaire de Paris centre - ED 406
École doctorale Sciences de la Décision, des Organisations, de la Société et de l’Echange (SDOSE) - ED 543
École doctorale Sciences fondamentales et appliquées - ED 364
École doctorale Sciences et technologies de l’information et de la communication - ED 84

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
Chemistry
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Inspired by international graduate schools, the Chemistry graduate programme brings together the strengths of PSL University at master's and doctoral level in various fields of chemistry, from its fundamentals to its applications. By networking the key players from the various PSL institutions (ENSCP – PSL, ENS – PSL, ESPCI Paris-PSL, Institut Curie and Collège de France), the Chemistry graduate programme develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalised study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalisation of curricula and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programmes strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programmes and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.
Key figures
185
Researchers and professors
100
Students in Master's programs
15
Laboratories
200
PhD students
Subjects
Physical and analytical approach at all scales
Theoretical chemistry and modelling
Materials Chemistry
Molecular design, synthesis and chemical biology
PHD Track
PSL PhD tracks | Join outstanding students from around the globe and launch your world-class research career with the PSL Graduate programme
The highly selective PhD tracks at PSL University prepare students for research careers in France or abroad, in academia or industry.
These five-year programmes are designed for high-potential students who want to take on the scientific challenges of tomorrow and prepare their research projects starting at the Master's level. Immersed in the ecosystem of excellence of PSL's Chemistry graduate programme (top-level laboratories and academic teams, international partners), successful applicants benefit from a personalised course of study geared towards their thesis topic from the first year of their Master's degree.
Why join the PSL PhD track?
Curriculum of excellence (Master's + PhD) accessible in M1
Funding for the entire duration of studies (5 years) & welcome package in year 1
Courses taught entirely in English
Close supervision & support for professional integration
Immersion in a multicultural & cutting-edge scientific environment in the heart of Paris
Find out more (admissions, grants, etc.)
Discover the PhD track & apply
Education
Research
Start with a master’s degree in chemistry with customized curricula
Master's degree in Chemistry
Personalized course offerings
Complementary university-wide courses
Graduate program students at both the Master’s and PhD level have access to university-wide trainings in core competencies, both introductory and advanced, designed to supplement their academic study.
Those complementary university-wide courses, available in all of PSL’s component schools, are eligible for ECTS credit.

FORMATION
Doctorat
Doctorat
Mines Paris – PSL propose un environnement unique pour un doctorat au meilleur niveau scientifique avec des liens privilégiés avec l’entreprise.
Une formation en trois ans au sein d’un laboratoire de Mines Paris, offrant ainsi une immersion complète dans le milieu académique et permettant au doctorant d’acquérir des compétences scientifiques tout en élargissant sa compréhension du monde économique.
Le doctorat en chiffres
100
docteurs diplômés par an
10
unités de recherche basées à Paris, Versailles-Satory, Fontainebleau, Sophia-Antipolis
230
enseignants-chercheurs « impliqués dans l’encadrement de thèses »
30%
de thèses CIFRE
30%
de femmes
40%
d’étudiants internationaux
Un doctorat de l’Université PSL préparé à Mines Paris – PSL
Le Collège doctoral de PSL réunit les écoles doctorales dont l’accréditation ou la co-accréditation est portée par l’université PSL. Mines Paris – PSL dispense la formation par la recherche alliant haut niveau scientifique et pragmatisme économique.
Les atouts
un diplôme de doctorat de l’Université PSL (première université française dans les classements internationaux THE et QS) préparé dans un centre de recherche de Mines Paris, reconnu sur la scène internationale;
une immersion dans un écosystème de recherche et d’innovation de pointe;
un accès à un large éventail de disciplines;
un accès à une communauté de recherche internationale et pluridisciplinaire;
un plan de formation personnalisé;
des actions de valorisation de votre doctorat
un tremplin pour votre carrière de docteur;
un accès au réseau des Alumni de Mines Paris.
5 écoles doctorales
Les écoles doctorales rattachées à l’école de Mines Paris – PSL :
SFA – Sciences Fondamentales et Appliquées – n° 364
ISMME – Ingénierie des Systèmes, Matériaux, Mécanique, Energétique n° 621
STIC – Sciences et technologies de l’information et de la communication – n° 84
SDOSE – Sciences de la Décision, des Organisations, de la Société et de l’Echange – n° 543
GRNE – Géosciences, Ressources Naturelles et Environnement – n° 39
Mines Paris – PSL est responsable de l’École doctorale Ingénierie des Systèmes, Matériaux, Mécanique, Énergétique (ISMME) au sein du collège doctoral de PSL, offrant aux doctorants un encadrement scientifique de haut niveau ainsi qu’une préparation à l’insertion professionnelle.

Une formation doctorale sur-mesure
Un plan de formation personnalisé en fonction du projet professionnel
La formation doctorale de Mines Paris – PSL est conçue pour répondre aux besoins spécifiques du projet professionnel du doctorant. Le programme offre une approche personnalisée, guidant les futurs docteurs vers des carrières dans le monde entier grâce à des formations transversales et professionnalisantes, pour répondre aux défis socio-économiques contemporains.
Pendant les trois années de recherche, les doctorants, encadrés par un directeur de thèse, sont pleinement intégrés dans une équipe et un centre de recherche qui participe activement à ses activités scientifiques, de recherche contractuelle et d’enseignement.
Offres de sujets de thèse
Une valorisation du doctorat
Nos doctorants suivent un programme de formation composé de modules variés pour traiter leur projet de thèse et préparer la poursuite de leur carrière :
des modules d’approfondissement scientifique ou technique en lien avec leur domaine de recherche
des modules transverses axés sur les compétences professionnelles telles que la connaissance du monde économique, la communication, la gestion d’équipe et l’insertion professionnelle
des modules d’ouverture pour une vision globale et interdisciplinaire
des cours de langues, comprenant l’anglais et le français pour les non-francophones, pour une communication efficace dans un contexte international
Soutenances de thèse à venir
Postuler
Conditions d’admission en doctorat
Pour être éligible en doctorat, le candidat devra démontrer qu’il détient un diplôme de master ou de niveau équivalent au moment de démarrer son doctorat.
Tous les doctorants sont financés pendant leur thèse. L’obtention d’un financement est indispensable pour l’inscription.
Pour être admis en doctorat, le candidat doit trouver un directeur de thèse habilité à diriger des recherches (HDR) avec qui il s’accorde sur un projet visant à être intégré dans une unité ou une équipe de recherche affiliée à une école doctorale accréditée ou co-accréditée par PSL.
À cet égard, il peut consulter la liste des projets de thèse ouverts à la candidature ou contacter directement un enseignant-chercheur qui travaille sur un sujet qui l’intéresse afin d’échanger ensemble sur les possibilités d’initier un doctorat.
Le directeur de thèse identifié pourra ensuite guider le candidat afin de trouver un financement pour toute la durée du doctorat et déposer son dossier de candidature.
Ce dossier doit comporter :
Une lettre de motivation
Un curriculum vitae
Un relevé de notes du cursus antérieur qui contient impérativement un stage de recherche (master recherche et/ou cycle ingénieur)
Une lettre de recommandation à minima
Une attestation du niveau d’anglais
Une preuve de financement
Contact :
doctorat@minesparis.psl.eu
Après une évaluation scientifique effectuée par les centres de recherche, les candidats sont sélectionnés lors d’entretiens avec un jury. Ces oraux visent à évaluer les candidats sur leur aptitude personnelle et leur adéquation avec le sujet de thèse proposé. Le recrutement est proposé par le responsable de la spécialité doctorale après approbation du jury de sélection et du directeur du centre de recherche.
Inscription à l’issue du processus d’admission
L’inscription se fait en ligne sur la plateforme ADUM et les documents relatifs aux procédures d’inscription et de réinscription sont disponibles dans l’espace ressources.
Tout savoir sur le Doctorat

Gérer le consentement aux cookies
Pour offrir les meilleures expériences, nous utilisons des technologies telles que les cookies pour stocker et/ou accéder aux informations des appareils. Le fait de consentir à ces technologies nous permettra de traiter des données telles que le comportement de navigation ou les ID uniques sur ce site. Le fait de ne pas consentir ou de retirer son consentement peut avoir un effet négatif sur certaines caractéristiques et fonctions.
Fonctionnel
Fonctionnel
Toujours activé
Le stockage ou l’accès technique est strictement nécessaire dans la finalité d’intérêt légitime de permettre l’utilisation d’un service spécifique explicitement demandé par l’abonné ou l’internaute, ou dans le seul but d’effectuer la transmission d’une communication sur un réseau de communications électroniques.
Préférences
Préférences
Le stockage ou l’accès technique est nécessaire dans la finalité d’intérêt légitime de stocker des préférences qui ne sont pas demandées par l’abonné ou la personne utilisant le service.
Statistiques
Statistiques
Le stockage ou l’accès technique qui est utilisé exclusivement à des fins statistiques.
Le stockage ou l’accès technique qui est utilisé exclusivement dans des finalités statistiques anonymes. En l’absence d’une assignation à comparaître, d’une conformité volontaire de la part de votre fournisseur d’accès à internet ou d’enregistrements supplémentaires provenant d’une tierce partie, les informations stockées ou extraites à cette seule fin ne peuvent généralement pas être utilisées pour vous identifier.
Marketing
Marketing
Le stockage ou l’accès technique est nécessaire pour créer des profils d’internautes afin d’envoyer des publicités, ou pour suivre l’internaute sur un site web ou sur plusieurs sites web ayant des finalités marketing similaires.

PSL Université Paris
Paris Sciences et Lettres, une université de rang mondial, au cœur de Paris
Connexion Espace personnel
Ce site est optimisé pour Google Chrome ou Mozilla Firefox. Merci d'utiliser un de ces navigateurs.

Important : Si vous êtes chercheur, nous vous invitons à consulter la Foire aux Questions (FAQ)
Intranet
L'espace personnel ADUM est l'espace unique dédié à toutes les démarches de demande ou de validation d'inscription, de réinscription et de soutenance de thèse.
Il permet :
d'accéder au dossier qui regroupe toutes les informations relatives au doctorat
de modifier ou rectifier les données vous concernant
de déposer des pièces administratives nécessaires à l'organisation du doctorat
d'accéder aux services du réseau ADUM :
 offres d'emploi
 actualités du doctorat
d'enrichir votre profil de compétences
de vous inscrire aux formations
d'assurer la diffusion en ligne des thèses sur theses.fr
L'ADUM est un outil de gestion et une base de données partagés entre les acteurs des études doctorales : doctorants, docteurs, chercheurs, direction de thèses, direction de laboratoire, direction d'Ecole doctorale, gestionnaires administratifs et pédagogiques des études/écoles doctorales, responsable de bibliothèque, direction recherche, Collège Doctoral.
La qualité des données présentes dans l'ADUM est certifiée par les personnels habilités des établissements utilisant l'outil. Les données sont gérées exclusivement par des personnels de l'établissement dédiés à cette mission.
Cookies : En vous connectant vous transmettez un ou plusieurs cookies à votre ordinateur (ou autre appareil).
Nous utilisons ces cookies uniquement pour faciliter votre navigation.
Ces cookies ne sont pas conservés et ne sont pas exploités et ne servent qu'à gérer les sessions, ils sont détruits au redémarrage du navigateur.
En cas de problème technique, vous pouvez nous contacter à l'adresse suivante : webmaster@adum.fr
Vous pouvez également consulter la FAQ concernant l'espace personnel des doctorants.
Pour toute autre demande, nous vous invitons à contacter directement l'école doctorale concernée ou à consulter leur site web.

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
PSL PhD Track: FAQ
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Highly selective and entirely taught in English, PSL PhD tracks are 5-year fully funded programs intended for high-potential students hoping to become top level researchers, in France or abroad.
Check out this FAQ to find out all the details and specifics of these programs of excellence (Master's + PhD).
Admissions & prerequisites
What is the difference between a Master’s degree and a PhD Track?
The Master’s degree is a two-year program
The PhD Track is a five-year, funded and research-oriented program, including a Master’s degree* and research internships, then a Doctorate. You will obtain a Master’s degree, then a PhD degree. These 2 diplomas will be awarded by Université PSL.
* When you apply to the PhD Track, you will be asked to choose the Master’s course that you want to follow for the first 2 years of the course (see list of eligible Master’s degrees).
Can I apply to the 2 programs, Master’s degree and PhD Track?
Absolutely. Given the very high selectivity of the PhD Tracks, it is strongly recommended that you apply for the Master’s degree in parallel if you are interested in both programs. Look at the page of the Master’s degree that you are interested in for the application calendar and how to apply.
What is the difference between a PhD Track and a graduate program?
The PhD Track is a 5-year course associated with a PSL graduate program.
The graduate programs follow the model of the “graduate school” in international universities, and consist of an entity (School) that contains research laboratories, academic teams, training programs, etc. The PhD tracks are specific training courses within a graduate program.
What does a “research-oriented course” mean?
The PSL PhD Tracks are intended for high-potential students hoping to become top-level researchers in the public or private sector.
If you are passionate about research and you want an international career, you are ideal for the PhD track program.
What are the admission criteria?
The admission criteria common to all the PSL PhD Tracks are as follows: academic excellence, research project, command of English (C1 level).
How are the social criteria assessed for obtaining a grant? Do I have to provide proof of my financial situation?
No personal proof is required for this purpose. The admission criteria are exclusively academic.
Is there an age limit for applying to the PhD track?
No.
I don’t speak French, can I still apply?
Certainly, if you speak English fluently. All courses are taught in English.
What level of English is required to apply? Do I have to provide proof?
Level C1 is strongly recommended. Supporting documents are not compulsory for all PhD tracks (see what documents are required in the application portal). Note that interviews with eligible candidates take place in English and allow the jury to assess your oral level.
I only have a Bachelor’s degree. Can I apply?
Certainly. However, note that many applicants already have a Master’s degree.
I have not yet graduated from my current course. Can I still apply?
Yes, if you are preparing the last year of an undergraduate diploma in the year you apply for PhD Tracks. You should upload the most recent transcripts you have available to the applications portal. If you have graduated, you will then be asked to produce your diploma when you enroll.
Admission procedure
What documents are required to apply?
Academic CV
Transcripts and diplomas from each higher education institution / university attended
One unique pdf for all documents. You can merge them with pdf-merge for example.
Statement of purpose (In English, strict 1 page limit)
The purpose of this document is to explain who you are and what you expect from your postgraduate studies. Give details about your interest in the field and your career goals. You can use this document to clarify anything unusual in your CV or your transcripts. One page maximum.
Research statement (In English, 1 to 2 pages)
This project should clearly define your interests within your area of research and specialization. You should present a structured research project showing your commitment and describing what you plan for the future of your research if you join a PSL PhD track.
Proof of English proficiency, level C1 (proof not compulsory but recommended)
The language prerequisites specific to each PhD track are detailed on the applications portal. In general:
- If your first language is English or if you have earned a higher education diploma (BA/BS.c, MA) in an English-speaking university, you should include a statement to this effect.
- If not, you should include the official results from a recent test of English, e.g. TOEFL or IELTS. If you have no recent official results, you can submit some older test results provisionally. This will not detract from the assessment of your application, but we reserve the right to ask for more recent official results later in the selection process.
References: contact details (name, position title, email) of at least two academic referees.
What is a research project?
The research project must be written in English and not exceed 2 pages.
A research project is a short document that provides a brief history of your past research experience, the current state of your research, and the future work you intend to complete. It should present the context of your work first, then show how it contributes to extend other ongoing work.
It should be written for an audience of other professionals in your field.
Make sure that your statement is clearly tied together. Show a progression.
What is the primary question that you have tried to address over the course of your internships and work experience? Why is this question important to the field? How has each stage of your work related to that question?
What is a statement of purpose?
The statement of purpose must be written in English and not exceed 1 page.
A statement of purpose (SOP), sometimes referred to as a personal statement, is a critical piece of a graduate program application. It tells admissions committees who you are, what your academic and professional interests are, and how you’ll add value to the GP you’re applying to.
The statement of purpose reflects not only who you are as a candidate but your writing abilities and qualifications as well.
A good personal statement should contain: your personal motivations for applying to the program, your accomplishments and success-stories, the challenges you’ve faced and overcome.
Tuition fees & Funding
How much are tuition fees?
The PhD tracks are five-year programs. As a general reference, the provisional annual enrollment fees listed below are based on the rates applicable for the 2026/27 academic year. Please note that these fees are not covered by the grant awarded to students enrolled in the PSL PhD tracks.
PSL PhD tracks
Master's degree (2 yrs)
Doctorate (3 yrs)
- PhD track in Computer science
- PhD track in Life sciences
- PhD track in Chemistry
- PhD track in Astrophysics
- For EU students: €255 / year
- For Non-EU students: € 3,950 / year
€398/ year (all nationalities)
In addition to these fees, students must pay the “Contribution to Student and Campus Life” (CVEC), which is approximately €105 per year. There are several exemptions available. Information
What is the amount of a PhD Track grant?
The PSL PhD Track grant is €10,000 / year at Master’s level then the equivalent of a doctoral contract based on French legislation.
Tuition fees, as well as the cost of housing, transport, and living expenses in general are paid by the student. See details on the website.
Is it possible to combine several grants?
The combining of funding with other grants will be examined on a case by case basis by the jury of the Graduate Program concerned, which will determine whether the 2 funding methods are compatible.
Note in particular that students who have also been awarded an Eiffel scholarship may not combine the 2 grants during the 2 years of the Master’s degree. However, the Eiffel scholarship can replace the Master’s grant provided in the PSL PhD track for the first two years of the program (until the start of the doctoral studies).
International students
Do you offer a welcome service for international students?
Certainly.
The PSL Welcome Desk & Euraxess Center assists international students and researchers across all PSL schools. Their team is available to help you prepare your arrival and manage non-academic administrative processes throughout your time with Université PSL. Please visit their web page for detailed information about entry requirements, residence permit, social security, health insurance, housing, and other daily life matters. The PSL Welcome Desk also offers social and cultural outings, including a Buddy Program and Tandem Partner Program.
Please feel free to schedule an appointment or email them with your questions!
The PSL Welcome Desk will help you at every stage, to advise you, guide you and ensure that your arrival in Paris and at PSL goes smoothly:
Before you arrive - For the main administrative formalities (visa, CVEC, transport, etc.) and put you in touch with other PSL students via the Buddy Program “Partner@PSL”.
When you arrive, come and see the Welcome Desk team during office hours to check out the next formalities that need to be completed: validation of your visa, registration with the French Social Security system, request for housing allowance from CAF, taking out home insurance, opening a bank account, etc.
During the university year: don’t hesitate to contact the PSL Welcome Desk for help with medical reimbursements or with finding a bilingual doctor. The international tutors can also give you a hand with finding a student job and following up on the renewal of your residence permit with the Prefecture.
Do you offer courses in French as a foreign language (FLE)?
Non-French speaking students can indeed follow FLE courses as part of their training at PSL. You will get all the information you need at the start of the academic year, at your host school.

October 14 - December 14, 2026

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
Mathematics and Applications
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Inspired by international graduate schools, the Mathematics and Applications graduate programme brings together the strengths of PSL University at master's and doctoral level. By networking the actors from the various PSL institutions (Dauphine-PSL, ENS-PSL, Mines-PSL, Collège de France, Observatoire de Paris-PSL), the PR[AI]RIE institute and PSAI, the Mathematics and Applications graduate programme develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalised study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalisation of courses and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programmes strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programmes and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.

A five-year track from Master’s to PhD
Upon entering the Master’s degree program, each student will define their scientific project with the graduate program's faculty and researchers. Students will have an academic advisor and will be heavily involved in research throughout the program.

Personalized course offerings
Complementary university-wide courses
Graduate program students at both the Master’s and PhD level have access to university-wide trainings in core competencies, both introductory and advanced, designed to supplement their academic study.
Those complementary university-wide courses, available in all of PSL’s component schools, are eligible for ECTS credit.

à propos de l'ED3C
L’Ecole Doctorale Cerveau, cognition, comportement (ED3C), fait l'objet d'une co-accréditation de Sorbonne Université (établissement porteur), de l'Université Paris Cité (UPC) et de l'Université Paris Sciences et Lettres (PSL). Elle regroupe des équipes de neurosciences et de sciences cognitives de ces 3 établissements, ainsi que de quelques autres établissements (Institut Pasteur, Université Paris-Saclay, etc.).

Slide précédentSlide suivant
Une formation d’excellence en informatique, entre décision et intelligence artificielle
Le programme doctoral en informatique s’appuie sur le laboratoire d’Analyse et de Modélisation de Systèmes pour l’Aide à la Décision (LAMSADE) et a pour objectif la formation de docteurs en informatique autour de domaines liés à la décision.
Le programme doctoral a une orientation pluridisciplinaire.
Ses axes de recherche sont très variés : Sciences de la décision ; Algorithmes pour la décision individuelle (mono- ou multi-critères), collective, coopérative, stratégique ; Aide à la décision, modélisation et élicitation des préférences ; Recherche Opérationnelle ; Optimisation combinatoire et applications ; Optimisation pour l’IA ; Sciences des Données ; Bases de données, systèmes d’information, gestion de données massives ; Intelligence artificielle, apprentissage automatique, apprentissage profond ; Grands modèles de langages ; Apprentissage par renforcement ; Applications de l’IA à la biologie et la médecine.
Les objectifs de la formation :
Proposer une formation spécifique en informatique mais également une préparation à l’embauche
Contribuer au suivi des travaux de thèse des doctorants
Offrir un lieu d’échange et de présentation des travaux en cours
Consolider et développer notre politique internationale (cotutelles internationales)
Renforcer nos liens avec les entreprises (CIFRE)
Entreprendre une thèse en Informatique
Trouver une direction de thèse
Un programme doctoral associé au laboratoire d’Analyse et de Modélisation de Systèmes pour l’Aide à la Décision
Le LAMSADE est un centre établi en 1974. Les thèmes de recherches originels de l’Aide à la décision et de la Recherche Opérationnelle ont ensuite été complétés par l’Informatique Décisionnelle, la Théorie de la Décision et l’Intelligence Artificielle.
Le LAMSADE propose des solutions pour la conception, l’utilisation et la validation de modèles formels d’Aide à la Décision. Les recherches commencent en amont des processus de décision, passent par l’extraction, l’apprentissage et la modélisation des données, des valeurs, des préférences, la conception des méthodes et systèmes (qui peuvent montrer une certaine autonomie et/ou intelligence) jusqu’à l’étude des aspects algorithmiques sous-jacents, et finissent en aval avec la validation et l’insertion des recommandations dans des contextes organisationnels réels.
Le LAMSADE est connu pour être à l’origine d’une démarche unique dans le domaine de l’aide multicritère à la décision, pour son approche à l’algorithmique et l’optimisation et pour la création du domaine de l’"Algorithmic Decision Theory". Dans tous ces domaines, il est parmi les leaders au niveau international.
La recherche menée au sein du LAMSADE est appliquée à des domaines extrêmement variés comme la planification des transports, l’ordonnancement, l’évaluation des appels d’offres, le confort dans les voitures des trains, l’acceptabilité sociale des nouvelles technologies d’hydrogène ou encore les réseaux des télécommunications.
Laboratoire de recherche LAMSADE
Programme de la formation doctorale
Dans le cadre de leur formation doctorale, il est demandé aux doctorants en informatique de :
Assister au séminaire des doctorants chaque année et d'y faire un exposé au moins une fois durant leur thèse.
Consacrer 27 heures de formation par an, comprenant nécessairement un cours, complété de séminaires ou exposés en lien avec leur activité de recherche. Le cours peut être proposé par le programme informatique de l'ED SDOSE, ou un cours de master.
Effectuer une formation à l'éthique scientifique une fois durant leur thèse.
Les doctorants en informatique sont encouragés à participer à une école thématique en lien avec leur sujet de recherche, de préférence en début de thèse.
Cursus et pré-requis
Le programme regroupe des doctorants issus des deux Masters Recherche suivants :
Master MODO - Modélisation, Optimisation, Décision et Organisation
Master IASD - Intelligence Artificielle, Systèmes, Données
Le programme accueille également des doctorants venant d’autres universités, avec une proportion importante d’étudiants ayant fait leurs études à l’étranger. Plusieurs accords de co-tutelle internationale existent (Tunisie, Grèce, Sénégal, Portugal, Italie), et l’École cherche activement à développer sa politique internationale.
Membres habilités à diriger des recherches
Trouver un directeur ou une directrice de thèse
Pour effectuer une thèse, il faut impérativement obtenir l'accord d'un membre habilité à diriger des recherches. Pour contacter un directeur de recherche cliquez directement sur son nom dans la liste ci-dessous ou consulter la CVthèque de Dauphine.

Axes scientifiques et laboratoires d’accueil

En savoir plus
Parcours du doctorant
Le parcours doctoral au sein de l’École Doctorale ISMME est structuré pour offrir un accompagnement rigoureux tout au long de la formation, conçu pour garantir une expérience académique et professionnelle complète.
Sujets de thèse, candidatures et recrutement
Découvrez les sujets de thèse proposés par l’École Doctorale ISMME et rejoignez une équipe de recherche avec un financement garanti pour trois ans.

Gestion des cookies
© 2026 Ecole doctorale ISMME Mines Paris – PSL
Gérer le consentement
Pour offrir les meilleures expériences, nous utilisons des technologies telles que les cookies pour stocker et/ou accéder aux informations des appareils. Le fait de consentir à ces technologies nous permettra de traiter des données telles que le comportement de navigation ou les ID uniques sur ce site. Le fait de ne pas consentir ou de retirer son consentement peut avoir un effet négatif sur certaines caractéristiques et fonctions.
Fonctionnel
Fonctionnel
Toujours activé
L’accès ou le stockage technique est strictement nécessaire dans la finalité d’intérêt légitime de permettre l’utilisation d’un service spécifique explicitement demandé par l’abonné ou l’utilisateur, ou dans le seul but d’effectuer la transmission d’une communication sur un réseau de communications électroniques.
Préférences
Préférences
L’accès ou le stockage technique est nécessaire dans la finalité d’intérêt légitime de stocker des préférences qui ne sont pas demandées par l’abonné ou l’internaute.
Statistiques
Statistiques
Le stockage ou l’accès technique qui est utilisé exclusivement à des fins statistiques.
Le stockage ou l’accès technique qui est utilisé exclusivement dans des finalités statistiques anonymes. En l’absence d’une assignation à comparaître, d’une conformité volontaire de la part de votre fournisseur d’accès à internet ou d’enregistrements supplémentaires provenant d’une tierce partie, les informations stockées ou extraites à cette seule fin ne peuvent généralement pas être utilisées pour vous identifier.
Marketing
Marketing
L’accès ou le stockage technique est nécessaire pour créer des profils d’internautes afin d’envoyer des publicités, ou pour suivre l’utilisateur sur un site web ou sur plusieurs sites web ayant des finalités marketing similaires.

Welcome Desk - Researchers
European University - EELISA
Schools
Earth Science and Biodiversity
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Inspired by international graduate schools, the Physics graduate program brings together the strengths of PSL University at the master's and doctoral levels in physics. By networking the actors of the various PSL institutions (ESPCI Paris-PSL, ENS-PSL, Observatoire de Paris-PSL, Institut Curie, Collège de France), the Physics graduate program develops a variety of initiatives: training through research and personalized study paths, interdisciplinarity, and the internationalization of curricula and career opportunities. Backed by the best laboratories and teams at PSL University, the graduate programs strengthen the links between master's and doctoral programs and create new opportunities for student and scientific communities.
Key figures
135
Researchers and professors
130
Students in Master's programs
15
Laboratories
130
PhD students
Subjects
Ecology
Biodiversity
Geophysical environment
Geochemical environment
Humanities
PHD Track
PSL PhD tracks | Join outstanding students from around the globe & launch your world-class research career with PSL Graduate program
PSL University's highly selective PhD Tracks prepare students for research careers in France or abroad, in academia or industry.
These five-year programs are designed for high-potential students who want to tackle the scientific challenges of tomorrow and begin preparing their research projects while still in their master's program. Immersed in the ecosystem of excellence of PSL's graduate physics program (top-level laboratories and academic teams, international partners), successful applicants benefit from a personalized course of study focused on their thesis topic from the first year of their master's degree.
Why join the PSL PhD track?
Curriculum of excellence (Master's + PhD) accessible in M1
Funding for the entire duration of studies (5 years) & welcome package in year 1
Courses taught entirely in English
Close supervision & support for professional integration
Immersion in a multicultural & cutting-edge scientific environment in the heart of Paris
More informations
Discover & apply to PhD track
Education
Research
Choose your Master’s degree and define your academic path
The graduate program offers the following Master’s degrees:
Master's degree in Life Sciences
Master's degree in Earth and planetary science, Environment
Personalized course offerings
Complementary university-wide courses
Graduate program students at both the Master’s and PhD level have access to university-wide trainings in core competencies, both introductory and advanced, designed to supplement their academic study.
Those complementary university-wide courses, available in all of PSL’s component schools, are eligible for ECTS credit.

Career opportunities
Trained in and through research, PSL alumni find numerous employment opportunities in the public, private and entrepreneurial sectors as well as in the academic world.
Pursue a PhD
Doctoral research is conducted in a PSL laboratory. Training is conducted in a doctoral school.

Gestion des cookies
Vos paramètres de cookies
Vous naviguez sur le site de l’Université PSL. L’Université PSL, responsable de traitement, utilise des cookies pour la réalisation de statistiques ainsi que pour l’optimisation du site. Vous pouvez accepter ou refuser l’utilisation de cookies, ainsi que personnaliser vos préférences. Nous conservons votre choix pendant 3 mois. Vous pouvez changer d’avis à tout moment en cliquant sur l’icône « Gestion des cookies » en bas à gauche sur toutes les pages de notre site internet.

Schools
PSL's Graduate programs
Home
Education
PSL's Graduate programs
Share :
Facebook
Linked in
Print
Following the model of graduate schools in the world’s top universities, the graduate programs are unprecedented curricula among higher education institutions in France. PSL’s graduate programs offer top-level training at Master’s and doctorate levels, based on the scientific resources of all component schools included in PSL. When you join a graduate program, you can choose:
A major
Optional minors
Elective courses to customize your curriculum
Your training will be based on the most recent breakthrough made in PSL’s laboratories and those of our international partners. You will be able to design your career plan to work either in the research field (academic or industrial), private sector or entrepreneurship.
Graduate programs in all the fields of study
From M1 year, you will be offered immersion in the research institutions by the contact with the best PSL’s researchers and laboratories. Through our partnerships with the economic sector and top international universities, you will benefit from numerous opportunities for mobility and integration.
An international and customizable curriculum
You are offered English tracks, internships, international mobility, inclusion in the PSL community of students and researchers… PSL’s Graduate programs are a gateway to internationalization.
To broaden your curriculum and improve your skills, you have access to elective and transdisciplinary courses.
Innovation
Career development/Soft skills
Scientific culture
Language (foreign languages, rare languages)

Gérer le consentement
Pour offrir les meilleures expériences, nous utilisons des technologies telles que les cookies pour stocker et/ou accéder aux informations des appareils. Le fait de consentir à ces technologies nous permettra de traiter des données telles que le comportement de navigation ou les ID uniques sur ce site. Le fait de ne pas consentir ou de retirer son consentement peut avoir un effet négatif sur certaines caractéristiques et fonctions.

About the ED 388
The 388 doctoral school, first attached to Sorbonne University, brings together the best teams from Paris Center, active in the fields of modern physical - and analytical chemistry. It brings together higher education and research establishments with laboratories in the area ofParis.

14 janvier 2026: Journée d' accueil des nouveaux doctorants
Nous accueillerons tous les nouveaux inscrits à l'ED SMAER le mercredi 14 janvier à 15h30 en salle 211 tour 55-65 (campus Pierre et Marie Curie).
À propos de l'ED 391
L’école doctorale Sciences Mécaniques, Acoustique, Électronique, et Robotique de Paris (ED SMAER) s’est donnée pour mission de faire émerger un champ sciences de l’ingénieur sur le site parisien, et de participer à la visibilité des sciences de l’ingénieur dans la région Île-de-France.

Candidature
Candidature
École Doctorale SDOSE
Un environnement académique d’exception à la fois
exigeant, stimulant et propice à l’innovation scientifique
L'École
Programmes doctoraux
Candidature
Mon doctorat
Présentation
L'École doctorale SDOSE offre une formation doctorale d’excellence en lien avec les grands enjeux contemporains
Elle regroupe sept programmes doctoraux disciplinaires et s’inscrit pleinement dans la dynamique des Programmes Gradués de PSL.
Commune à l'Université Paris Dauphine-PSL et MINES Paris PSL, l'École doctorale est portée par l’Université PSL et rattachée au Collège doctoral de PSL.
L'École doctorale c'est :
Un accompagnement personnalisé : une formation à la recherche exigeante et ouverte
Un environnement académique de haut niveau : accès privilégié aux ressources académiques et aux événements scientifiques de PSL
Une recherche connectée à l’international : des opportunités de collaboration avec la communauté scientifique internationale
Chiffres clés
341
doctorants inscrits
85
doctorants en première année
105
doctorants de nationalité étrangère
58
soutenances de thèse
7 programmes
doctoraux
L'École doctorale SDOSE offre des formations doctorales de haut niveau structurées autour de domaines clés et associés aux laboratoires de recherche de l'Université Paris Dauphine-PSL ou de Mines Paris PSL
Les programmes combinent enseignements approfondis, séminaires méthodologiques et accompagnement personnalisé par des chercheurs reconnus. La formation prépare efficacement aux carrières académiques ou professionnelles, en favorisant notamment l'ouverture internationale et les coopérations avec les entreprises.

Whether in the fundamental sciences, engineering and applied sciences, humanities and social sciences, arts and literature, environmental sciences, or management science, a PSL doctorate is a path of excellence within our dissertation preparation schools featuring unique support provided by the Doctoral College within a world-renowned research-intensive university.
